from pathlib import Path
import re, sys
root=Path('/mnt/data/work_v3')

def read_sql(name):
    b=(root/name).read_bytes()
    for enc in ('utf-8-sig','gb18030'):
        try: return b.decode(enc)
        except UnicodeDecodeError: pass
    raise RuntimeError(name)

sql01=read_sql('01_create_tables.sql')
sql02=read_sql('02_insert_sample_data.sql')
sql03=read_sql('03_crud.sql')
sql06=read_sql('06_constraint.sql')
sql07=read_sql('07_role.sql')
md2=(root/'第二周 数据库设计.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
md3=(root/'第三周 脚本说明.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
md4=(root/'第四周 设计说明.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
todo=(root/'后续阶段待实现业务规则.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')

errors=[]
def req(cond,msg):
    if not cond: errors.append(msg)

# Required new schema constraints.
required_constraints=[
 'CK_SalesOrder_StatusTime','CK_PresaleReservation_CancelInfo',
 'UQ_PresaleReservation_ReservationMember','FK_SalesOrder_ReservationMember',
 'CK_PurchaseOrder_ReceivedInfo','CK_PresaleActivity_PickupDeadline',
 'CK_PresaleActivity_DepositNotExceedPrice','CK_PurchaseOrder_ExpectedArrival',
 'CK_PurchaseOrder_ReceivedTime'
]
for c in required_constraints:
    req(re.search(r'CONSTRAINT\s+'+re.escape(c)+r'\b',sql01,re.I) is not None, f'missing constraint {c}')

# Constraint names unique in create script.
names=re.findall(r'CONSTRAINT\s+([A-Za-z0-9_]+)',sql01,re.I)
for n in set(names):
    if names.count(n)>1: errors.append(f'duplicate constraint name {n}')

# Test expectations all point to real schema constraints, except fourth-week added refund constraint.
expected=re.findall(r"LIKE\s+N'%([A-Za-z0-9_]+)%'",sql06,re.I)
defs=set(names)|set(re.findall(r'ADD\s+CONSTRAINT\s+([A-Za-z0-9_]+)',sql06,re.I))
for n in expected:
    req(n in defs, f'test expects undefined constraint {n}')

# T1-T14 all present.
for i in range(1,15):
    req(f'T{i}：' in sql06, f'missing T{i}')

# Known precedence-sensitive tests.
req("9210, 'SO_BAD_MEMBER', 'PRESALE', 1, 1,\n        2, NULL, 'CREATED'" in sql06, 'T10 not using R002/member mismatch as intended')
req("'PO_BAD_RECEIVE_INFO'" in sql06 and "'ORDERED'" in sql06, 'T12 missing')

# CRUD ordering: item quantity update must precede order COMPLETED update.
pos_item=sql03.find('UPDATE dbo.SalesOrderItem')
pos_complete=sql03.find("UPDATE dbo.SalesOrder\nSET\n    order_status = 'COMPLETED'")
req(0 <= pos_item < pos_complete, 'CRUD update order wrong')

# T3 must have exactly 8 values in its INSERT block (baseline _2 had an extra value).
m=re.search(r"T3：重复商品业务编号.*?INSERT INTO dbo\.Product\s*\((.*?)\)\s*VALUES\s*\((.*?)\);",sql06,re.S)
req(m is not None,'cannot parse T3')
if m:
    cols=[x.strip() for x in m.group(1).split(',') if x.strip()]
    # No commas inside T3 scalar values, so simple split is reliable here.
    vals=[x.strip() for x in m.group(2).split(',') if x.strip()]
    req(len(cols)==8 and len(vals)==8, f'T3 columns/values mismatch {len(cols)}/{len(vals)}')

# Documentation alignment.
req('Product : Inventory = 1 : 0..1' in md2,'inventory cardinality doc not fixed')
req('复合外键' in md2 and 'FK(reservation_id, member_id)' in md2,'reservation/member composite relation not documented')
req('0 <= deposit_per_unit <= presale_unit_price' in md2,'deposit enhancement not documented')
req('pickup_deadline > end_time' in md2,'pickup deadline not documented')
req('expected_arrival IS NULL OR expected_arrival >= order_time' in md2,'expected arrival rule not documented')
req('received_time IS NULL OR received_time >= order_time' in md2,'received time rule not documented')
req('普通现货订单可以包含多种商品' in md2,'sales item wording not fixed')
req('普通补货采购订单可以包含多种商品' in md2,'purchase item wording not fixed')
req('列级权限控制' in md4 and '合法状态迁移' in md4,'permission scope not clarified')
req('已由约束覆盖的典型非法记录' in md4,'final conclusion still overclaims')
req('后续阶段待实现业务规则.md' in md3 and '后续阶段待实现业务规则.md' in md4,'future-gap doc not referenced')

# Future-gap list contains all planned themes.
for term in ['预售开放期间','quantity × deposit_per_unit','截止前取消全额退订金','PICKED_UP',
             '预售销售订单商品','预售销售数量','预售成交价','预售采购应在预售截止后',
             'PRESALE` 采购明细商品','采购订单状态','完成销售时检查可售库存','采购验收后增加库存',
             'reserved_qty','经办销售员工','状态只能按照合法状态机迁移','会员只能访问和操作自己的订单']:
    req(term in todo, f'future-gap doc missing theme: {term}')

# Sample data compatibility with new constraints, checked from known sample tuples.
req("30.00,\n        0.00,\n        'PICKED_UP',\n        NULL" in sql02,'R001 sample unexpected')
req("30.00,\n        0.00,\n        'CANCELLED',\n        '2026-09-18T11:00:00'" in sql02,'R002 sample incompatible')
req("3, 'SO003',\n        'PRESALE',\n        1,\n        1,\n        1,\n        '2026-09-26T10:20:00',\n        'COMPLETED'" in sql02,'SO003 sample unexpected')

# Crude lexical balance checks after stripping comments/string literals for parentheses.
def strip_literals_comments(s):
    s=re.sub(r'/\*.*?\*/','',s,flags=re.S)
    s=re.sub(r'--.*','',s)
    s=re.sub(r"N?'(?:''|[^'])*'", "''", s)
    return s
for fn in ['01_create_tables.sql','06_constraint.sql']:
    txt=strip_literals_comments(read_sql(fn))
    req(txt.count('(')==txt.count(')'), f'unbalanced parentheses in {fn}: {txt.count("(")}/{txt.count(")")}')

if errors:
    print('VALIDATION FAILED')
    for e in errors: print('-',e)
    sys.exit(1)
print('VALIDATION PASSED')
print('constraints:', len(names), 'tests:', 14, 'future-rule document lines:', len(todo.splitlines()))
PY

+## 5.3 结构约束复核
+
+在阶段整合复核中，将几项能够直接用声明式约束表达的业务关系补回建表脚本，包括：
+
+- `COMPLETED` 销售订单必须记录 `order_time`；
+- `CANCELLED` 预订必须记录 `cancelled_at`，其他状态不记录取消时间；
+- 预售提货订单的会员必须与对应预订会员一致；
+- `RECEIVED` 采购订单与验收时间、验收员工保持一致；
+- 最晚提货期限晚于预售结束时间；
+- 每件订金不超过预售价；
+- 预计到货时间、实际验收时间不早于采购下单时间。
+
+这些规则均已在 `01_create_tables.sql` 中落实，并在本周反例测试中验证。仍需跨表流程或事务才能强制的规则统一记录在 `后续阶段待实现业务规则.md`。

| **4. 退款规则强于当前实现** | 不增加复杂跨表约束。第二、四周文档明确区分“当前保证退款金额基本合法”与“截止前全退/截止后不退需要后续跨表业务过程实现”。 |
| **5. 预售订单可能关联别人的预订** | 在 `PresaleReservation` 增加 `(reservation_id, member_id)` 辅助 UNIQUE，并在 `SalesOrder` 增加 `FK_SalesOrder_ReservationMember` 复合外键。特别避免把这个组合错误称为新的候选码。 |
| **6. PurchaseOrder 验收状态约束只有单向** | 重写 `CK_PurchaseOrder_ReceivedInfo`：`RECEIVED` 时验收时间和员工均非空；其他状态二者均为空。 |
| **7. RESTOCK 与 presale_id 的关系** | 按你的决策不收紧代码。文档改成“正常 RESTOCK 流程填写 NULL，但第一阶段只强制 PRESALE 必须关联活动”，严格化需求移入后续规则文档。 |
| **8. pickup_deadline 可早于预售结束时间** | 新增 `CK_PresaleActivity_PickupDeadline`，要求为空或晚于 `end_time`；文档同步。 |
| **9. 销售订单‘可包含多商品’表述过宽** | 第二周改为：普通现货订单可多商品；当前 PRESALE 按一次 reservation 独立提货，只应记录对应商品。商品/数量跨表强制匹配留到后续事务。 |
| **10. 采购订单‘可包含多商品’表述过宽** | 改为 RESTOCK 可以多商品；PRESALE 采购在当前模型中应对应本预售活动商品，强制匹配留到后续。 |
| **11. 销售店员预订状态权限过宽** | 第四周改成准确的“当前采用列级权限限制可修改范围”，不再暗示数据库已经限制所有合法状态迁移；状态机后续实现。 |
| **12. 库存管理员采购状态权限过宽** | 同样明确当前只控制可修改字段，采购状态合法迁移路径后续通过数据库编程控制。 |

| **14. Inventory.updated_at 语义过强** | 第二周改为“库存记录更新时间，由库存修改语句同步维护”，不再暗示 DBMS 自动维护。 |
| **增强 A：订金不可超过预售价** | 新增 `CK_PresaleActivity_DepositNotExceedPrice`，并同步第二周文档。 |

第三周文档也顺手与 `_2` 已修正的 CRUD 代码重新对齐为：

/* =========================================================
   00_create_database.sql
   二次元周边商品店数据库
   ========================================================= */

IF DB_ID(N'AnimeGoodsStoreDB') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE DATABASE AnimeGoodsStoreDB;
END;
GO

USE AnimeGoodsStoreDB;
GO
===== 01_create_tables.sql =====
/* =========================================================
   01_create_tables.sql
   二次元周边商品店：创建数据表与基础完整性约束

   执行前请先执行：
   00_create_database.sql
   ========================================================= */

USE AnimeGoodsStoreDB;
GO


/* =========================================================
   1. ProductCategory 商品类别
   ========================================================= */

CREATE TABLE dbo.ProductCategory
(
    category_id INT NOT NULL,
    category_name NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    description NVARCHAR(200) NULL,

    CONSTRAINT PK_ProductCategory
        PRIMARY KEY (category_id),

    CONSTRAINT UQ_ProductCategory_CategoryName
        UNIQUE (category_name),

    CONSTRAINT CK_ProductCategory_CategoryID
        CHECK (category_id > 0)
);
GO


/* =========================================================
   2. AnimeIP 作品 / IP
   ========================================================= */

CREATE TABLE dbo.AnimeIP
(
    ip_id INT NOT NULL,
    ip_name NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    description NVARCHAR(200) NULL,

    CONSTRAINT PK_AnimeIP
        PRIMARY KEY (ip_id),

    CONSTRAINT UQ_AnimeIP_IPName
        UNIQUE (ip_name),

    CONSTRAINT CK_AnimeIP_IPID
        CHECK (ip_id > 0)
);
GO


/* =========================================================
   3. BusinessRole 员工业务角色
   注意：这里是现实经营角色，不是 SQL Server 权限角色
   ========================================================= */

CREATE TABLE dbo.BusinessRole
(
    role_id INT NOT NULL,
    role_name NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    description NVARCHAR(200) NULL,

    CONSTRAINT PK_BusinessRole
        PRIMARY KEY (role_id),

    CONSTRAINT UQ_BusinessRole_RoleName
        UNIQUE (role_name),

    CONSTRAINT CK_BusinessRole_RoleID
        CHECK (role_id > 0)
);
GO


/* =========================================================
   4. Member 会员
   ========================================================= */

CREATE TABLE dbo.Member
(
    member_id INT NOT NULL,
    member_no VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    member_name NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    phone VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,

    register_time DATETIME2 NOT NULL
        CONSTRAINT DF_Member_RegisterTime
        DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),

    member_status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL
        CONSTRAINT DF_Member_Status
        DEFAULT ('ACTIVE'),

    CONSTRAINT PK_Member
        PRIMARY KEY (member_id),

    CONSTRAINT UQ_Member_MemberNo
        UNIQUE (member_no),

    CONSTRAINT UQ_Member_Phone
        UNIQUE (phone),

    CONSTRAINT CK_Member_MemberID
        CHECK (member_id > 0),

    CONSTRAINT CK_Member_Status
        CHECK (member_status IN ('ACTIVE', 'INACTIVE'))
);
GO


/* =========================================================
   5. Supplier 供应商
   ========================================================= */

CREATE TABLE dbo.Supplier
(
    supplier_id INT NOT NULL,
    supplier_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    supplier_name NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    contact_name NVARCHAR(50) NULL,
    phone VARCHAR(20) NULL,

    supplier_status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL
        CONSTRAINT DF_Supplier_Status
        DEFAULT ('ACTIVE'),

    CONSTRAINT PK_Supplier
        PRIMARY KEY (supplier_id),

    CONSTRAINT UQ_Supplier_SupplierCode
        UNIQUE (supplier_code),

    CONSTRAINT CK_Supplier_SupplierID
        CHECK (supplier_id > 0),

    CONSTRAINT CK_Supplier_Status
        CHECK (supplier_status IN ('ACTIVE', 'INACTIVE'))
);
GO


/* =========================================================
   6. CharacterInfo 角色
   ========================================================= */

CREATE TABLE dbo.CharacterInfo
(
    character_id INT NOT NULL,
    ip_id INT NOT NULL,
    character_name NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT PK_CharacterInfo
        PRIMARY KEY (character_id),

    -- 同一 IP 下角色名称不能重复
    CONSTRAINT UQ_CharacterInfo_IP_CharacterName
        UNIQUE (ip_id, character_name),

    -- 为 Product 中的复合外键提供候选键
    CONSTRAINT UQ_CharacterInfo_CharacterID_IP
        UNIQUE (character_id, ip_id),

    CONSTRAINT FK_CharacterInfo_AnimeIP
        FOREIGN KEY (ip_id)
        REFERENCES dbo.AnimeIP(ip_id),

    CONSTRAINT CK_CharacterInfo_CharacterID
        CHECK (character_id > 0)
);
GO


/* =========================================================
   7. Employee 员工
   ========================================================= */

CREATE TABLE dbo.Employee
(
    employee_id INT NOT NULL,
    employee_no VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    employee_name NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    role_id INT NOT NULL,
    phone VARCHAR(20) NULL,
    hire_date DATE NOT NULL,

    employee_status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL
        CONSTRAINT DF_Employee_Status
        DEFAULT ('ACTIVE'),

    CONSTRAINT PK_Employee
        PRIMARY KEY (employee_id),

    CONSTRAINT UQ_Employee_EmployeeNo
        UNIQUE (employee_no),

    CONSTRAINT FK_Employee_BusinessRole
        FOREIGN KEY (role_id)
        REFERENCES dbo.BusinessRole(role_id),

    CONSTRAINT CK_Employee_EmployeeID
        CHECK (employee_id > 0),

    CONSTRAINT CK_Employee_Status
        CHECK (employee_status IN ('ACTIVE', 'INACTIVE'))
);
GO


/* =========================================================
   8. Product 商品
   ========================================================= */

CREATE TABLE dbo.Product
(
    product_id INT NOT NULL,
    product_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    product_name NVARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,

    category_id INT NOT NULL,
    ip_id INT NULL,
    character_id INT NULL,

    sale_price DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,

    product_status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL
        CONSTRAINT DF_Product_Status
        DEFAULT ('ON_SALE'),

    created_at DATETIME2 NOT NULL
        CONSTRAINT DF_Product_CreatedAt
        DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),

    CONSTRAINT PK_Product
        PRIMARY KEY (product_id),

    CONSTRAINT UQ_Product_ProductCode
        UNIQUE (product_code),

    CONSTRAINT FK_Product_ProductCategory
        FOREIGN KEY (category_id)
        REFERENCES dbo.ProductCategory(category_id),

    CONSTRAINT FK_Product_AnimeIP
        FOREIGN KEY (ip_id)
        REFERENCES dbo.AnimeIP(ip_id),

    CONSTRAINT FK_Product_CharacterInfo
        FOREIGN KEY (character_id)
        REFERENCES dbo.CharacterInfo(character_id),

    -- 保证角色和 IP 属于同一个角色记录
    CONSTRAINT FK_Product_Character_IP
        FOREIGN KEY (character_id, ip_id)
        REFERENCES dbo.CharacterInfo(character_id, ip_id),

    CONSTRAINT CK_Product_ProductID
        CHECK (product_id > 0),

    CONSTRAINT CK_Product_SalePrice
        CHECK (sale_price > 0),

    CONSTRAINT CK_Product_Status
        CHECK (
            product_status IN
            ('PENDING', 'ON_SALE', 'STOPPED')
        ),

    -- 如果指定具体角色，就必须同时指定 IP
    CONSTRAINT CK_Product_CharacterRequiresIP
        CHECK (
            character_id IS NULL
            OR ip_id IS NOT NULL
        )
);
GO


/* =========================================================
   9. Inventory 库存
   ========================================================= */

CREATE TABLE dbo.Inventory
(
    product_id INT NOT NULL,

    on_hand_qty INT NOT NULL
        CONSTRAINT DF_Inventory_OnHandQty
        DEFAULT (0),

    reserved_qty INT NOT NULL
        CONSTRAINT DF_Inventory_ReservedQty
        DEFAULT (0),

    reorder_point INT NOT NULL
        CONSTRAINT DF_Inventory_ReorderPoint
        DEFA
...[truncated]...

PRINT N'========== P1：销售店员正常销售操作 ==========';

BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRAN;

    EXECUTE AS USER = 'u_test_sales';

    INSERT INTO dbo.SalesOrder
    (
        order_id,
        order_no,
        order_type,
        member_id,
        cashier_id,
        reservation_id,
        order_time,
        order_status
    )
    VALUES
    (
        9301,
        'SO_ROLE_TEST',
        'SPOT',
        1,
        1,
        NULL,
        NULL,
        'CREATED'
    );

    INSERT INTO dbo.SalesOrderItem
    (
        order_id,
        product_id,
        quantity,
        unit_price
    )
    VALUES
    (
        9301,
        1,
        1,
        68.00
    );

    UPDATE dbo.SalesOrder
    SET
        order_status = 'COMPLETED',
        order_time = SYSDATETIME()
    WHERE order_id = 9301;

    PRINT N'[PASS] P1：销售店员可以正常创建和完成销售订单。';

    REVERT;

    ROLLBACK TRAN;
END TRY

BEGIN CATCH

    IF USER_NAME() = N'u_test_sales'
        REVERT;

    IF XACT_STATE() <> 0
        ROLLBACK TRAN;

    PRINT N'[FAIL] P1：销售店员正常销售操作失败。';
    PRINT ERROR_MESSAGE();

END CATCH;
GO

/* =========================================================
   第八部分：越权操作测试
   ========================================================= */


/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   N1. 销售店员试图修改采购订单

   预期：
   权限不足，操作失败。
   --------------------------------------------------------- */

PRINT N'========== N1：销售店员越权修改采购订单 ==========';

BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRAN;

    EXECUTE AS USER = 'u_test_sales';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        N'
        UPDATE dbo.PurchaseOrder
        SET purchase_status = ''CANCELLED''
        WHERE purchase_id = 2;
        ';

    REVERT;

    PRINT N'[FAIL] N1：销售店员竟然可以修改采购订单。';

    ROLLBACK TRAN;
END TRY

BEGIN CATCH

    DECLARE @N1_ErrorNumber INT = ERROR_NUMBER();
    DECLARE @N1_ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(4000) = ERROR_MESSAGE();

    IF USER_NAME() = N'u_test_sales'
        REVERT;

    IF XACT_STATE() <> 0
        ROLLBACK TRAN;

    IF @N1_ErrorNumber = 229
       AND @N1_ErrorMessage LIKE N'%PurchaseOrder%'
    BEGIN
        PRINT N'[PASS] N1：销售店员修改 PurchaseOrder 因 UPDATE 权限不足被正确拒绝。';
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        PRINT N'[FAIL] N1：操作虽然失败，但失败原因不是预期的权限拒绝（错误号 229）。';
    END;

    PRINT N'错误号：' + CAST(@N1_ErrorNumber AS NVARCHAR(20));
    PRINT N'错误信息：' + @N1_ErrorMessage;

END CATCH;
GO

/* =========================================================
   第九部分：角色与成员检查
   ========================================================= */

SELECT
    r.name AS database_role,
    m.name AS database_user
FROM sys.database_role_members AS drm
INNER JOIN sys.database_principals AS r
    ON drm.role_principal_id = r.principal_id
INNER JOIN sys.database_principals AS m
    ON drm.member_principal_id = m.principal_id
WHERE r.name IN
(
    N'dbrole_sales_clerk',
    N'dbrole_inventory_manager',
    N'dbrole_store_manager'
)
ORDER BY r.name;
GO

 330: /* =========================================================
 331:    10. PresaleActivity 预售活动
 332:    ========================================================= */
 333: 
 334: CREATE TABLE dbo.PresaleActivity
 335: (
 336:     presale_id INT NOT NULL,
 337:     presale_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
 338: 
 339:     product_id INT NOT NULL,
 340: 
 341:     start_time DATETIME2 NOT NULL,
 342:     end_time DATETIME2 NOT NULL,
 343: 
 344:     presale_unit_price DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
 345:     deposit_per_unit DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
 346: 
 347:     pickup_deadline DATETIME2 NULL,
 348: 
 349:     decision_demand_qty INT NULL,
 350: 
 351:     extra_stock_qty INT NOT NULL
 352:         CONSTRAINT DF_PresaleActivity_ExtraStockQty
 353:         DEFAULT (0),
 354: 
 355:     planned_purchase_qty INT NULL,
 356: 
 357:     created_by INT NOT NULL,
 358: 
 359:     presale_status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL
 360:         CONSTRAINT DF_PresaleActivity_Status
 361:         DEFAULT ('PLANNED'),
 362: 
 363:     CONSTRAINT PK_PresaleActivity
 364:         PRIMARY KEY (presale_id),
 365: 
 366:     CONSTRAINT UQ_PresaleActivity_PresaleCode
 367:         UNIQUE (presale_code),
 368: 
 369:     CONSTRAINT FK_PresaleActivity_Product
 370:         FOREIGN KEY (product_id)
 371:         REFERENCES dbo.Product(product_id),
 372: 
 373:     CONSTRAINT FK_PresaleActivity_Employee
 374:         FOREIGN KEY (created_by)
 375:         REFERENCES dbo.Employee(employee_id),
 376: 
 377:     CONSTRAINT CK_PresaleActivity_PresaleID
 378:         CHECK (presale_id > 0),
 379: 
 380:     CONSTRAINT CK_PresaleActivity_Time
 381:         CHECK (start_time < end_time),
 382: 
 383:     CONSTRAINT CK_PresaleActivity_PickupDeadline
 384:         CHECK (
 385:             pickup_deadline IS NULL
 386:             OR pickup_deadline > end_time
 387:         ),
 388: 
 389:     CONSTRAINT CK_PresaleActivity_UnitPrice
 390:         CHECK (presale_unit_price > 0),
 391: 
 392:     CONSTRAINT CK_PresaleActivity_Deposit
 393:         CHECK (deposit_per_unit >= 0),
 394: 
 395:     CONSTRAINT CK_PresaleActivity_DepositNotExceedPrice
 396:         CHECK (deposit_per_unit <= presale_unit_price),
 397: 
 398:     CONSTRAINT CK_PresaleActivity_DecisionDemand
 399:         CHECK (
 400:             decision_demand_qty IS NULL
 401:             OR decision_demand_qty >= 0
 402:         ),
 403: 
 404:     CONSTRAINT CK_PresaleActivity_ExtraStock
 405:         CHECK (extra_stock_qty >= 0),
 406: 
 407:     CONSTRAINT CK_PresaleActivity_PlannedPurchase
 408:         CHECK (
 409:             planned_purchase_qty IS NULL
 410:             OR planned_purchase_qty >= 0
 411:         ),
 412: 
 413:     CONSTRAINT CK_PresaleActivity_Status

 415:             presale_status IN
 416:             (
 417:                 'PLANNED',
 418:                 'OPEN',
 419:                 'CLOSED',
 420:                 'PURCHASING',
 421:                 'ARRIVED',
 422:                 'FINISHED'
 423:             )
 424:         )
 425: );
 426: GO

 489:

 159: /* 验证库存状态视图 */
 160: 
 161: SELECT *
 162: FROM dbo.vw_InventoryStatus
 163: ORDER BY available_qty, product_id;
 164: GO
 165: 
 166: 
 167: 
 168: /* =========================================================
 169:    V4. 会员消费统计视图
 170: 
 171:    即使会员当前没有产生消费，也保留该会员，
 172:    completed_order_count 和 total_consumption 为 0。
 173:    ========================================================= */
 174: 
 175: CREATE VIEW dbo.vw_MemberConsumptionSummary
 176: AS
 177: SELECT
 178:     m.member_id,
 179:     m.member_no,
 180:     m.member_name,
 181: 
 182:     COUNT(DISTINCT so.order_id)
 183:         AS completed_order_count,
 184: 
 185:     COALESCE(
 186:         SUM(soi.quantity * soi.unit_price),
 187:         0
 188:     ) AS total_consumption
 189: 
 190: FROM dbo.Member AS m
 191: 
 192: LEFT JOIN dbo.SalesOrder AS so
 193:     ON m.member_id = so.member_id
 194:    AND so.order_status = 'COMPLETED'
 195: 
 196: LEFT JOIN dbo.SalesOrderItem AS soi
 197:     ON so.order_id = soi.order_id
 198: 
 199: GROUP BY
 200:     m.member_id,
 201:     m.member_no,
 202:     m.member_name;
 203: GO
 204: 
 205: 
 206: /* 验证会员消费统计视图 */
 207: 
 208: SELECT *
 209: FROM dbo.vw_MemberConsumptionSummary
 210: ORDER BY total_consumption DESC, member_id;
 211: GO

===== 06_constraint.sql =====
   1: /* =========================================================
   2:    06_constraint.sql
   3:    二次元周边商品店：完整性约束补充与验证
   4: 
   5:    执行前：
   6:    00_create_database.sql
   7:    01_create_tables.sql
   8:    02_insert_sample_data.sql
   9: 
  10:    本文件完成：
  11:    1. 补充必要的完整性约束
  12:    2. 合法数据正例测试
  13:    3. T1-T14 非法数据反例测试（仅因预期约束失败才判定 PASS）
  14: 
  15:    所有测试均使用事务并回滚，不修改正式样例数据。
  16:    ========================================================= */
  17: 
  18: USE AnimeGoodsStoreDB;
  19: GO
  20: 
  21: 
  22: /* =========================================================
  23:    第一部分：补充完整性约束
  24:    ========================================================= */
  25: 
  26: 
  27: /* ---------------------------------------------------------
  28:    PresaleReservation：
  29:    已退订金不能超过实际支付订金。
  30: 
  31:    原有约束已经分别保证：
  32:        deposit_paid >= 0
  33:        deposit_refunded >= 0
  34: 
  35:    这里进一步补充二者之间的关系：
  36:        deposit_refunded <= deposit_paid
  37:    --------------------------------------------------------- */
  38: 
  39: IF NOT EXISTS
  40: (
  41:     SELECT 1
  42:     FROM sys.check_constraints
  43:     WHERE name = N'CK_PresaleReservation_RefundNotExceedPaid'
  44:       AND parent_object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.PresaleReservation')
  45: )
  46: BEGIN
  47:     ALTER TABLE dbo.PresaleReservation
  48:     WITH CHECK
  49:     ADD CONSTRAINT CK_PresaleReservation_RefundNotExceedPaid
  50:         CHECK (deposit_refunded <= deposit_paid);
  51: END;
  52: GO
  53: 
  54: 
  55: 
  56: /* =========================================================
  57:    第二部分：合法数据正例
  58:    ========================================================= */
  59: 
  60: 
  61: /* ---------------------------------------------------------
  62:    正例 P1：
  63:    合法商品 + 合法库存能够正常插入。
  64: 
  65:    测试成功后回滚，不保留测试数据。
  66:    --------------------------------------------------------- */
  67: 
  68: PRINT N'========== Positive Test P1 ==========';
  69: 
  70: BEGIN TRY
  71:     BEGIN TRAN;
  72: 
  73:     INSERT INTO dbo.Product
  74:     (
  75:         product_id,
  76:         product_code,
  77:         product_name,
  78:         category_id,
  79:         ip_id,
  80:         character_id,
  81:         sale_price,
  82:         product_status
  83:     )
  84:     VALUES
  85:     (
  86:         9101,
  87:         'P_VALID_TEST',
  88:         N'合法约束测试商品',
  89:         1,
  90:         1,
  91:         1,
  92:         50.00,
  93:         'ON_SALE'
  94:     );
  95: 
  96:     INSERT INTO dbo.Inventory
  97:     (
  98:         product_id,
  99:         on_hand_qty,
 100:         reserved_qty,
 101:         reorder_point
 102:     )
 103:     VALUES
 104:     (
 105:         9101,
 106:         10,
 107:         3,
 108:         5
 109:     );
 110: 
 111:     PRINT N'[PASS] P1：合法商品和库存数据插入成功。';
 112: 
 113:     ROLLBACK TRAN;
 114: END TRY
 115: 
 116: BEGIN CATCH
 117:     IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
 118:         ROLLBACK TRAN;
 119: 
 120:     PRINT N'[FAIL] P1：合法数据被错误拒绝。';

===== 07_role.sql =====
   1: /* =========================================================
   2:    07_role.sql
   3:    二次元周边商品店：数据库角色与权限验证
   4: 
   5:    执行前：
   6:    00_create_database.sql
   7:    01_create_tables.sql
   8:    02_insert_sample_data.sql
   9:    04_query.sql
  10:    05_view.sql
  11:    06_constraint.sql
  12: 
  13:    本文件：
  14:    1. 创建三个数据库角色
  15:    2. 按最小权限原则授权
  16:    3. 创建 WITHOUT LOGIN 测试用户
  17:    4. 验证正常操作成功
  18:    5. 验证越权操作仅因权限不足失败时才判定 PASS
  19:    ========================================================= */
  20: 
  21: USE AnimeGoodsStoreDB;
  22: GO
  23: 
  24: 
  25: /* =========================================================
  26:    第一部分：创建数据库角色
  27:    ========================================================= */
  28: 
  29: IF DATABASE_PRINCIPAL_ID(N'dbrole_sales_clerk') IS NULL
  30:     EXEC(N'CREATE ROLE dbrole_sales_clerk AUTHORIZATION dbo;');
  31: GO
  32: 
  33: IF DATABASE_PRINCIPAL_ID(N'dbrole_inventory_manager') IS NULL
  34:     EXEC(N'CREATE ROLE dbrole_inventory_manager AUTHORIZATION dbo;');
  35: GO
  36: 
  37: IF DATABASE_PRINCIPAL_ID(N'dbrole_store_manager') IS NULL
  38:     EXEC(N'CREATE ROLE dbrole_store_manager AUTHORIZATION dbo;');
  39: GO
  40: 
  41: 
  42: 
  43: /* =========================================================
  44:    第二部分：销售 / 收银店员权限
  45:    ========================================================= */
  46: 
  47: /*
  48:    可以：
  49:    - 查看商品、库存状态
  50:    - 查看和维护会员
  51:    - 查看预售、预订
  52:    - 创建和处理销售订单
  53:    - 确认预售提货状态
  54: 
  55:    不可以：
  56:    - 修改库存
  57:    - 管理供应商
  58:    - 创建采购订单
  59:    - 管理员工
  60: */
  61: 
  62: 
  63: /* 商品查询 */
  64: GRANT SELECT
  65: ON OBJECT::dbo.Product
  66: TO dbrole_sales_clerk;
  67: GO
  68: 
  69: 
  70: /* 使用库存状态视图，不直接修改 Inventory */
  71: GRANT SELECT
  72: ON OBJECT::dbo.vw_InventoryStatus
  73: TO dbrole_sales_clerk;
  74: GO
  75: 
  76: 
  77: /* 查看订单详情 */
  78: GRANT SELECT
  79: ON OBJECT::dbo.vw_OrderDetail
  80: TO dbrole_sales_clerk;
  81: GO
  82: 
  83: 
  84: /* 会员查询、登记和修改 */
  85: GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE
  86: ON OBJECT::dbo.Member
  87: TO dbrole_sales_clerk;
  88: GO
  89: 
  90: 
  91: /* 查看预售活动 */
  92: GRANT SELECT
  93: ON OBJECT::dbo.PresaleActivity
  94: TO dbrole_sales_clerk;
  95: GO
  96: 
  97: 
  98: /* 查看预订 */
  99: GRANT SELECT
 100: ON OBJECT::dbo.PresaleReservation
 101: TO dbrole_sales_clerk;
 102: GO
 103: 
 104: 
 105: /*
 106:    当前阶段允许店员修改预订状态，
 107:    用于确认预售提货等业务。
 108: */
 109: GRANT UPDATE (reservation_status)
 110: ON OBJECT::dbo.PresaleReservation
 111: TO dbrole_sales_clerk;
 112: GO
 113: 
 114: 
 115: /* 创建销售订单 */
 116: GRANT INSERT
 117: ON OBJECT::dbo.SalesOrder
 118: TO dbrole_sales_clerk;
 119: GO
 120:

/* =========================================================
   03_crud.sql

   CRUD 演示：
   1. 商品
   2. 库存
   3. 销售订单及订单明细

   执行前：
   00_create_database.sql
   01_create_tables.sql
   02_insert_sample_data.sql
   ========================================================= */

USE AnimeGoodsStoreDB;
GO


/* =========================================================
   第一部分：商品与库存 CRUD
   ========================================================= */


/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   C = CREATE / INSERT

   新增一个测试商品
   --------------------------------------------------------- */

INSERT INTO dbo.Product
(
    product_id,
    product_code,
    product_name,
    category_id,
    ip_id,
    character_id,
    sale_price,
    product_status
)
VALUES
(
    9001,
    'P_TEST',
    N'CRUD测试商品',
    1,
    NULL,
    NULL,
    20.00,
    'ON_SALE'
);
GO


/* 为新商品建立库存记录 */

INSERT INTO dbo.Inventory
(
    product_id,
    on_hand_qty,
    reserved_qty,
    reorder_point
)
VALUES
(
    9001,
    10,
    0,
    3
);
GO


/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   R = READ / SELECT
   --------------------------------------------------------- */

SELECT
    p.product_id,
    p.product_code,
    p.product_name,
    p.sale_price,
    p.product_status,
    i.on_hand_qty,
    i.reserved_qty,
    i.on_hand_qty - i.reserved_qty AS available_qty,
    i.reorder_point
FROM dbo.Product AS p
JOIN dbo.Inventory AS i
    ON p.product_id = i.product_id
WHERE p.product_id = 9001;
GO


/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   U = UPDATE

   修改前先使用相同 WHERE 条件进行 SELECT。
   --------------------------------------------------------- */

SELECT *
FROM dbo.Product
WHERE product_id = 9001;
GO


UPDATE dbo.Product
SET
    sale_price = 22.00,
    product_name = N'CRUD测试商品（修改后）'
WHERE product_id = 9001;
GO


SELECT *
FROM dbo.Product
WHERE product_id = 9001;
GO


/* 库存更新 */

SELECT *
FROM dbo.Inventory
WHERE product_id = 9001;
GO


UPDATE dbo.Inventory
SET
    on_hand_qty = 15,
    reorder_point = 5,
    updated_at = SYSDATETIME()
WHERE product_id = 9001;
GO


SELECT
    *,
    on_hand_qty - reserved_qty AS available_qty
FROM dbo.Inventory
WHERE product_id = 9001;
GO


/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   D = DELETE

   Product 被 Inventory 引用，因此不能直接先删 Product。
   先确认待删除数据。
   --------------------------------------------------------- */

SELECT *
FROM dbo.Inventory
WHERE product_id = 9001;

SELECT *
FROM dbo.Product
WHERE product_id = 9001;
GO


/* 先删除子表 */

DELETE FROM dbo.Inventory
WHERE product_id = 9001;
GO


/* 再删除父表 */

DELETE FROM dbo.Product
WHERE product_id = 9001;
GO


/* 确认已经删除 */

SELECT *
FROM dbo.Product
WHERE product_id = 9001;

SELECT *
FROM dbo.Inventory
WHERE product_id = 9001;
GO



/* =========================================================
   第二部分：销售订单 CRUD
   ========================================================= */


/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   C = CREATE

   创建一笔普通现货测试订单。
   --------------------------------------------------------- */

INSERT INTO dbo.SalesOrder
(
    order_id,
    order_no,
    order_type,
    member_id,
    cashier_id,
    reservation_id,
    order_time,
    order_status
)
VALUES
(
    9001,
    'SO_TEST',
    'SPOT',
    NULL,
    1,
    NULL,
    NULL,
    'CREATED'
);
GO


/* 添加订单明细 */

INSERT INTO dbo.SalesOrderItem
(
    order_id,
    product_id,
    quantity,
    unit_price
)
VALUES
(
    9001,
    1,
    1,
    68.00
);
GO


/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   R = READ

   联表查询这笔测试订单。
   --------------------------------------------------------- */

SELECT
    so.order_id,
    so.order_no,
    so.order_type,
    so.order_status,
    p.product_name,
    soi.quantity,
    soi.unit_price,
    soi.quantity * soi.unit_price AS item_amount
FROM dbo.SalesOrder AS so
JOIN dbo.SalesOrderItem AS soi
    ON so.order_id = soi.order_id
JOIN dbo.Product AS p
    ON soi.product_id = p.product_id
WHERE so.order_id = 9001;
GO


/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   U = UPDATE

   在订单完成前先修改并确认订单明细。
   --------------------------------------------------------- */

SELECT *
FROM dbo.SalesOrderItem
WHERE order_id = 9001
  AND product_id = 1;
GO


UPDATE dbo.SalesOrderItem
SET quantity = 2
WHERE order_id = 9001
  AND product_id = 1;
GO


SELECT
    order_id,
    product_id,
    quantity,
    unit_price,
    quantity * unit_price AS item_amount
FROM dbo.SalesOrderItem
WHERE order_id = 9001
  AND product_id = 1;
GO


/* 确认明细后，将订单由 CREATED 变为 COMPLETED */

SELECT *
FROM dbo.SalesOrder
WHERE order_id = 9001;
GO


UPDATE dbo.SalesOrder
SET
    order_status = 'COMPLETED',
    order_time = SYSDATETIME()
WHERE order_id = 9001;
GO


SELECT *
FROM dbo.SalesOrder
WHERE order_id = 9001;
GO


/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   D = DELETE

   SalesOrderItem 引用了 SalesOrder，
   所以仍然按照子表 → 父表顺序删除。
   --------------------------------------------------------- */

SELECT *
FROM dbo.SalesOrderItem
WHERE order_id = 9001;

SELECT *
FROM dbo.SalesOrder
WHERE order_id = 9001;
GO


DELETE FROM dbo.SalesOrderItem
WHERE order_id = 9001;
GO


DELETE FROM dbo.SalesOrder
WHERE order_id = 9001;
GO


/* 最终验证 */

SELECT *
FROM dbo.SalesOrder
WHERE order_id = 9001;

SELECT *
FROM dbo.SalesOrderItem
WHERE order_id = 9001;
GO

我选择的兼容策略是：**不删除整个数据库，也不新增 reset 文件；把 `01_create_tables.sql` 设计成“模式重建入口”**。也就是说，`00` 在数据库不存在时创建、存在时复用；`01` 会先按依赖顺序删除本项目已有视图和业务表，再重新创建全部表。这样第一次运行和“完整链第二次运行”走完全相同的 `00 → 01 → … → 07` 顺序，同时不会把危险的 `DROP DATABASE` 藏进普通建库脚本。

此外 `05_view.sql` 会改成 `CREATE OR ALTER VIEW`，使视图脚本本身也可以安全重复运行；`06`、`07` 原本已有存在性判断/重复授权兼容逻辑，会保留。

------

## 三、SQL 脚本组成

第三周目前包含四个主要 SQL 文件。

### 1. `00_create_database.sql`

用于创建数据库：

```
AnimeGoodsStoreDB
```

如果数据库已经存在，则不会重复创建。

脚本完成数据库创建后切换到该数据库，为后续建表做好准备。

### 2. `01_create_tables.sql`

根据第二周确定的关系模式创建全部 15 张数据表。

脚本中实现了以下主要完整性约束：

- `PRIMARY KEY`：保证每张表中的主要标识唯一；
- `UNIQUE`：保证商品编号、订单编号、会员编号等业务编号不重复；
- `FOREIGN KEY`：保证不同表之间引用的数据真实存在；
- `NOT NULL`：保证必要业务字段必须填写；
- `DEFAULT`：为状态、时间和部分数值字段设置合理默认值；
- `CHECK`：限制价格、数量、状态等字段的合法取值范围。

例如：

- 商品售价必须大于 0；
- 库存数量不能为负数；
- 预留库存不能超过实际库存；
- 销售数量和采购数量必须大于 0；
- 实际到货数量不能超过采购数量；
- 商品状态只能取 `PENDING`、`ON_SALE` 或 `STOPPED`；
- 订单状态只能取规定的状态值。

此外，对商品的 IP 和角色关系进行了组合约束，防止出现角色与所属 IP 不一致的数据。

对于销售订单中的 `reservation_id`，普通现货订单允许其为 `NULL`，而同一条非空预售预订最多只能对应一笔预售提货订单，因此使用了过滤唯一索引实现这一规则。

### 3. `02_insert_sample_data.sql`

用于向数据库中插入样例数据。
received_qty >= 0
received_qty <= ordered_qty
reserved_qty >= 0
reserved_qty <= on_hand_qty
```

这些规则都能够直接通过 `CHECK` 约束表达。

同时，主键、候选码和外键也在数据库中实际建立，而不是仅在设计文档中描述。

部分规则需要跨表比较、状态迁移或库存联动，不适合在第一阶段通过简单声明式约束强行实现。当前由样例数据和业务流程保持一致，后续计划结合事务、存储过程和触发器等内容实现；完整清单统一记录在 `后续阶段待实现业务规则.md` 中。

------

## 六、脚本执行顺序

从空数据库环境开始时，应按照以下顺序执行：

```
00_create_database.sql
        ↓
01_create_tables.sql
        ↓
02_insert_sample_data.sql
        ↓
03_crud.sql
```

各脚本作用分别为：

```
创建数据库
        ↓
创建表和约束
        ↓
导入业务样例数据
        ↓
执行 CRUD 测试
```

目前已按照上述顺序实际执行全部脚本。

全部脚本均正常完成，执行过程中未出现 SQL 报错，也未发现明显的数据关系或约束冲突。

因此目前数据库能够完成从数据库创建、表结构建立、样例数据导入到基础数据修改操作的完整流程。

------

## 七、本周实验结果

经过实际运行，目前已完成：

- `AnimeGoodsStoreDB` 数据库创建；
- 15 张业务数据表创建；
- 主键、唯一约束、外键、默认值和检查约束创建；
- 商品、库存、会员、员工、供应商、预售、采购和销售样例数据导入；
- 商品新增、查询、修改和删除；
- 库存新增、查询、修改和删除；
- 销售订单及订单明细新增、查询、修改和删除；
- 外键相关删除顺序验证；
- 全部第三周 SQL 脚本按顺序执行。
因此补充：

```
deposit_refunded <= deposit_paid
```

对应约束：

```
CK_PresaleReservation_RefundNotExceedPaid
```

该约束只负责保证退款金额的基本数值关系；截止前全额退款、截止后不退款需要跨表比较预售截止时间，留到后续业务过程统一处理。

该约束用于防止出现例如：

```
实际支付订金 = 20
实际退款金额 = 30
```

这样的明显不合理数据。

------

## 5.3 结构约束复核

在阶段整合复核中，将几项能够直接用声明式约束表达的业务关系补回建表脚本，包括：

- `COMPLETED` 销售订单必须记录 `order_time`；
- `CANCELLED` 预订必须记录 `cancelled_at`，其他状态不记录取消时间；
- 预售提货订单的会员必须与对应预订会员一致；
- `RECEIVED` 采购订单与验收时间、验收员工保持一致；
- 最晚提货期限晚于预售结束时间；
- 每件订金不超过预售价；
- 预计到货时间、实际验收时间不早于采购下单时间。

这些规则均已在 `01_create_tables.sql` 中落实，并在本周反例测试中验证。仍需跨表流程或事务才能强制的规则统一记录在 `后续阶段待实现业务规则.md`。

from pathlib import Path
root=Path('/mnt/data/v4work')

# 00: clarify create-or-reuse behavior.
p=root/'00_create_database.sql'
s=p.read_bytes().decode('gb18030')
s='''/* =========================================================\r\n   00_create_database.sql\r\n   二次元周边商品店数据库\r\n\r\n   兼容首次运行与完整链重复运行：\r\n   - 数据库不存在：创建 AnimeGoodsStoreDB\r\n   - 数据库已存在：直接复用数据库\r\n   - 业务表 / 视图的重建由 01_create_tables.sql 负责\r\n   ========================================================= */\r\n\r\nIF DB_ID(N'AnimeGoodsStoreDB') IS NULL\r\nBEGIN\r\n    CREATE DATABASE AnimeGoodsStoreDB;\r\n    PRINT N'AnimeGoodsStoreDB 已创建。';\r\nEND\r\nELSE\r\nBEGIN\r\n    PRINT N'AnimeGoodsStoreDB 已存在，将复用现有数据库。';\r\nEND;\r\nGO\r\n\r\nUSE AnimeGoodsStoreDB;\r\nGO\r\n'''
p.write_bytes(s.encode('gb18030'))

# 01: add deterministic rebuild block after USE/GO.
p=root/'01_create_tables.sql'
s=p.read_bytes().decode('gb18030')
old='''USE AnimeGoodsStoreDB;\r\nGO\r\n\r\n\r\n/* =========================================================\r\n   1. ProductCategory 商品类别\r\n'''
block='''USE AnimeGoodsStoreDB;\r\nGO\r\n\r\n\r\n/* =========================================================\r\n   0. 重建准备\r\n\r\n   为同时兼容首次运行和已有数据库上的完整链重复运行，\r\n   本脚本先删除本项目已有视图，再按外键依赖逆序删除\r\n   15 张业务表，随后重新创建。\r\n\r\n   注意：重新执行本脚本会清空这些业务表中的现有数据。\r\n   本行为用于课程实验的可复现重建，不应用于生产数据库。\r\n   ========================================================= */\r\n\r\nDROP VIEW IF EXISTS dbo.vw_MemberConsumptionSummary;\r\nDROP VIEW IF EXISTS dbo.vw_InventoryStatus;\r\nDROP VIEW IF EXISTS dbo.vw_ProductSalesSummary;\r\nDROP VIEW IF EXISTS dbo.vw_OrderDetail;\r\nGO\r\n\r\nDROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.SalesOrderItem;\r\nDROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.SalesOrder;\r\nDROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.PurchaseOrderItem;\r\nDROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.PurchaseOrder;\r\nDROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.PresaleReservation;\r\nDROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.PresaleActivity;\r\nDROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.Inventory;\r\nDROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.Product;\r\nDROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.Employee;\r\nDROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.CharacterInfo;\r\nDROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.Supplier;\r\nDROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.Member;\r\nDROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.BusinessRole;\r\nDROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.AnimeIP;\r\nDROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.ProductCategory;\r\nGO\r\n\r\n\r\n/* =========================================================\r\n   1. ProductCategory 商品类别\r\n'''
if old not in s:
    raise SystemExit('01 insertion anchor not found')
s=s.replace(old,block,1)
p.write_bytes(s.encode('gb18030'))

# 05: standalone repeatable view creation.
p=root/'05_view.sql'
s=p.read_bytes().decode('gb18030')
s=s.replace('CREATE VIEW dbo.vw_OrderDetail','CREATE OR ALTER VIEW dbo.vw_OrderDetail')
s=s.replace('CREATE VIEW dbo.vw_ProductSalesSummary','CREATE OR ALTER VIEW dbo.vw_ProductSalesSummary')
s=s.replace('CREATE VIEW dbo.vw_InventoryStatus','CREATE OR ALTER VIEW dbo.vw_InventoryStatus')
s=s.replace('CREATE VIEW dbo.vw_MemberConsumptionSummary','CREATE OR ALTER VIEW dbo.vw_MemberConsumptionSummary')
# update header if anchor exists
s=s.replace('''   执行前请先执行：\r\n''','''   视图使用 CREATE OR ALTER，可在表结构存在时安全重复执行。\r\n\r\n   执行前请先执行：\r\n''',1)
p.write_bytes(s.encode('gb18030'))

# Third-week documentation: light edits for rerun behavior.
p=root/'第三周 脚本说明.md'
s=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
s=s.replace('''如果数据库已经存在，则不会重复创建。\n\n脚本完成数据库创建后切换到该数据库，为后续建表做好准备。''',
'''如果数据库已经存在，则直接复用现有数据库，不重复创建。业务表和视图的重建由下一步 `01_create_tables.sql` 统一处理。\n\n脚本完成数据库创建或复用后切换到该数据库，为后续建表做好准备。''')
s=s.replace('''### 2. `01_create_tables.sql`\n\n根据第二周确定的关系模式创建全部 15 张数据表。''',
'''### 2. `01_create_tables.sql`\n\n根据第二周确定的关系模式创建全部 15 张数据表。为了兼容已有数据库上的完整链重复运行，脚本会先删除本项目已有的统计视图，再按外键依赖的逆序删除 15 张业务表，随后重新创建表结构。\n\n因此该脚本既可以用于首次建表，也可以作为课程实验中的“模式重建入口”。重新执行会清空这些业务表中的现有数据，以保证后续样例数据和测试从确定的初始状态开始。''')
s=s.replace('''## 六、脚本执行顺序\n\n从空数据库环境开始时，应按照以下顺序执行：''',
'''## 六、脚本执行顺序\n\n首次运行以及已有数据库上的完整链重复运行，都按照以下顺序执行：''')
s=s.replace('''创建数据库\n        ↓\n创建表和约束\n        ↓\n导入业务样例数据\n        ↓\n执行 CRUD 测试''',
'''创建或复用数据库\n        ↓\n重建表结构和基础约束\n        ↓\n导入业务样例数据\n        ↓\n执行 CRUD 测试''')
s=s.replace('''目前已按照上述顺序实际执行全部脚本。\n\n全部脚本均正常完成，执行过程中未出现 SQL 报错，也未发现明显的数据关系或约束冲突。\n\n因此目前数据库能够完成从数据库创建、表结构建立、样例数据导入到基础数据修改操作的完整流程。''',
'''当前执行链采用确定性重建方式：数据库不存在时由 `00` 创建；数据库已经存在时由 `00` 复用，随后 `01` 重建本项目表结构。这样无需额外的 reset 脚本，同一套执行顺序即可覆盖首次运行和完整链再次运行。\n\n`02_insert_sample_data.sql` 和 `03_crud.sql` 仍按阶段链使用，不设计为脱离 `01` 后在任意已有业务数据状态下单独反复初始化；需要重新得到课程样例初始状态时，应从 `00 → 01` 开始完整执行。\n\n因此数据库能够完成从数据库创建/复用、表结构重建、样例数据导入到基础数据修改操作的完整流程。''')
p.write_text(s,encoding='utf-8')

# Fourth-week documentation: add concise reproducibility section before final summary if possible.
p=root/'第四周 设计说明.md'
s=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
insert='''\n## 十二、阶段复现与重复运行\n\n第一阶段完整复现统一按照：\n\n```\n00_create_database.sql\n→ 01_create_tables.sql\n→ 02_insert_sample_data.sql\n→ 03_crud.sql\n→ 04_query.sql\n→ 05_view.sql\n→ 06_constraint.sql\n→ 07_role.sql\n```\n\n执行。`00` 在数据库不存在时创建数据库、存在时直接复用；`01` 会删除本项目已有视图和业务表并按依赖关系重新建表，因此完整执行链可以在已有数据库上再次运行并回到确定的样例初始状态。`05` 使用 `CREATE OR ALTER VIEW`，`06` 的补充约束带存在性判断，`07` 的角色、测试用户和角色成员关系也带存在性判断或可重复授权逻辑。\n\n这里的重复运行是课程实验的“完整重建”语义：重新执行 `01` 会清空当前业务表数据。若只是希望保留已有业务数据，不应从 `01` 开始重建。\n\n------\n\n'''
# Renumber existing 十二/十三 if present. inspect headings.
import re
heads=[(m.start(),m.group(0)) for m in re.finditer(r'^# .*|^## .*',s,re.M)]
# Insert before final major conclusion heading if '# 十三' exists; else before last '#'.
anchor='# 十三、'
if anchor in s:
    s=s.replace('# 十三、','# 十四、',1)
    # any ## underneath no need
    s=s.replace('# 十二、', '# 十三、',1) if '# 十二、' in s else s
# Better find known last heading exact
for candidate in ['# 十三、实验总结','# 十二、实验总结','# 十一、实验总结']:
    if candidate in s:
        s=s.replace(candidate, insert+candidate.replace('十三','十四').replace('十二','十三').replace('十一','十二'),1)
        break
else:
    # append before last 100 lines? Just append section before end summary not critical
    s += '\n'+insert
p.write_text(s,encoding='utf-8')

print('patched')

### 00_create_database.sql
  1: /* =========================================================
  2:    00_create_database.sql
  3:    二次元周边商品店数据库
  4: 
  5:    兼容首次运行与完整链重复运行：
  6:    - 数据库不存在：创建 AnimeGoodsStoreDB
  7:    - 数据库已存在：直接复用数据库
  8:    - 业务表 / 视图的重建由 01_create_tables.sql 负责
  9:    ========================================================= */
 10: 
 11: IF DB_ID(N'AnimeGoodsStoreDB') IS NULL
 12: BEGIN
 13:     CREATE DATABASE AnimeGoodsStoreDB;
 14:     PRINT N'AnimeGoodsStoreDB 已创建。';
 15: END
 16: ELSE
 17: BEGIN
 18:     PRINT N'AnimeGoodsStoreDB 已存在，将复用现有数据库。';
 19: END;
 20: GO
 21: 
 22: USE AnimeGoodsStoreDB;
 23: GO

### 01_create_tables.sql
  1: /* =========================================================
  2:    01_create_tables.sql
  3:    二次元周边商品店：创建数据表与基础完整性约束
  4: 
  5:    执行前请先执行：
  6:    00_create_database.sql
  7:    ========================================================= */
  8: 
  9: USE AnimeGoodsStoreDB;
 10: GO
 11: 
 12: 
 13: /* =========================================================
 14:    0. 重建准备
 15: 
 16:    为同时兼容首次运行和已有数据库上的完整链重复运行，
 17:    本脚本先删除本项目已有视图，再按外键依赖逆序删除
 18:    15 张业务表，随后重新创建。
 19: 
 20:    注意：重新执行本脚本会清空这些业务表中的现有数据。
 21:    本行为用于课程实验的可复现重建，不应用于生产数据库。
 22:    ========================================================= */
 23: 
 24: DROP VIEW IF EXISTS dbo.vw_MemberConsumptionSummary;
 25: DROP VIEW IF EXISTS dbo.vw_InventoryStatus;
 26: DROP VIEW IF EXISTS dbo.vw_ProductSalesSummary;
 27: DROP VIEW IF EXISTS dbo.vw_OrderDetail;
 28: GO
 29: 
 30: DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.SalesOrderItem;
 31: DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.SalesOrder;
 32: DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.PurchaseOrderItem;
 33: DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.PurchaseOrder;
 34: DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.PresaleReservation;
 35: DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.PresaleActivity;
 36: DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.Inventory;
 37: DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.Product;
 38: DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.Employee;
 39: DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.CharacterInfo;
 40: DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.Supplier;
 41: DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.Member;
 42: DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.BusinessRole;
 43: DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.AnimeIP;
 44: DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dbo.ProductCategory;
 45: GO
 46: 
 47: 
 48: /* =========================================================
 49:    1. ProductCategory 商品类别
 50:    ========================================================= */
 51: 
 52: CREATE TABLE dbo.ProductCategory
 53: (
 54:     category_id INT NOT NULL,
 55:     category_name NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
 56:     description NVARCHAR(200) NULL,
 57: 
 58:     CONSTRAINT PK_ProductCategory
 59:         PRIMARY KEY (category_id),
 60: 
 61:     CONSTRAINT UQ_ProductCategory_CategoryName
 62:         UNIQUE (category_name),
 63: 
 64:     CONSTRAINT CK_ProductCategory_CategoryID
 65:         CHECK (category_id > 0)
 66: );
 67: GO
 68: 
 69: 
 70: /* =========================================================
 71:    2. AnimeIP 作品 / IP
 72:    ========================================================= */
 73: 
 74: CREATE TABLE dbo.AnimeIP
 75: (
 76:     ip_id INT NOT NULL,
 77:     ip_name NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
 78:     description NVARCHAR(200) NULL,
 79: 
 80:     CONSTRAINT PK_AnimeIP
 81:         PRIMARY KEY (ip_id),
 82: 
 83:     CONSTRAINT UQ_AnimeIP_IPName
 84:         UNIQUE (ip_name),
 85: 
 86:     CONSTRAINT CK_AnimeIP_IPID
 87:         CHECK (ip_id > 0)
 88: );
 89: GO
 90: 
 91: 
 92: /* =========================================================
 93:    3. BusinessRole 员工业务角色
 94:    注意：这里是现实经营角色，不是 SQL Server 权限角色
 95:    ========================================================= */

### 05_view.sql
  1: /* =========================================================
  2:    05_view.sql
  3:    二次元周边商品店：第四周统计视图
  4: 
  5:    执行前：
  6:    00_create_database.sql
  7:    01_create_tables.sql
  8:    02_insert_sample_data.sql
  9:    ========================================================= */
 10: 
 11: USE AnimeGoodsStoreDB;
 12: GO
 13: 
 14: 
 15: /* =========================================================
 16:    V1. 订单详情视图
 17:    ========================================================= */
 18: 
 19: CREATE OR ALTER VIEW dbo.vw_OrderDetail
 20: AS
 21: SELECT
 22:     so.order_id,
 23:     so.order_no,
 24:     so.order_type,
 25:     so.order_status,
 26:     so.order_time,
 27: 
 28:     so.member_id,
 29:     m.member_no,
 30:     m.member_name,
 31: 
 32:     so.cashier_id,
 33:     e.employee_no AS cashier_no,
 34:     e.employee_name AS cashier_name,
 35: 
 36:     soi.product_id,
 37:     p.product_code,
 38:     p.product_name,
 39: 
 40:     soi.quantity,
 41:     soi.unit_price,
 42:     soi.quantity * soi.unit_price AS item_amount
 43: 
 44: FROM dbo.SalesOrder AS so
 45: INNER JOIN dbo.SalesOrderItem AS soi
 46:     ON so.order_id = soi.order_id
 47: INNER JOIN dbo.Product AS p
 48:     ON soi.product_id = p.product_id
 49: INNER JOIN dbo.Employee AS e
 50:     ON so.cashier_id = e.employee_id
 51: LEFT JOIN dbo.Member AS m
 52:     ON so.member_id = m.member_id;
 53: GO
 54: 
 55: 
 56: /* 验证订单详情视图 */
 57: 
 58: SELECT *
 59: FROM dbo.vw_OrderDetail
 60: ORDER BY order_id, product_id;
 61: GO
 62: 
 63: 
 64: 
 65: /* =========================================================
 66:    V2. 商品销售统计视图
 67: 
 68:    仅 COMPLETED 订单计入实际销量和销售额。
 69:    没有产生销量的商品仍保留，统计值为 0。
 70:    ========================================================= */
 71: 
 72: CREATE OR ALTER VIEW dbo.vw_ProductSalesSummary
 73: AS
 74: SELECT
 75:     p.product_id,
 76:     p.product_code,
 77:     p.product_name,
 78: 
 79:     SUM(
 80:         CASE
 81:             WHEN so.order_status = 'COMPLETED'
 82:             THEN soi.quantity
 83:             ELSE 0
 84:         END
 85:     ) AS total_sales_qty,
 86: 
 87:     SUM(
 88:         CASE
 89:             WHEN so.order_status = 'COMPLETED'
 90:             THEN soi.quantity * soi.unit_price
 91:             ELSE 0
 92:         END
 93:     ) AS total_sales_amount
 94: 
 95: FROM dbo.Product AS p

PresaleReservation
```

表级查询权限后，会员可能获得查看其他会员数据的能力，与“只能访问本人数据”的业务要求冲突。

因此当前阶段将会员视为应用层业务角色：

```
会员
→ 后续通过应用程序或更细粒度访问机制访问本人数据
```

而不是：

```
会员
→ 直接登录数据库并查询基础表
```

第四周 SQL Server 权限设计主要针对店铺内部员工。

------

# 十三、当前第四周实验结果

经过本周设计和人工抽样核验，目前已完成：

- 多表销售订单详情查询；
- 商品累计销量与销售额统计；
- IP 销售统计；
- 商品类别销售统计；
- 会员消费统计；
- 库存不足查询；
- 预售有效需求统计；
- `HAVING` 聚合筛选查询；
- 子查询；
- 订单详情视图；
- 商品销售统计视图；
- 库存状态视图；
- 会员消费统计视图；
- 预售订金退款完整性约束补充；
- 合法数据正例测试；
- 十四组非法数据反例测试，并按预期错误号与约束名判定测试结果；
- 三个内部员工数据库角色；
- 基于最小权限原则的对象和列级授权；
- 三个无登录测试用户；
- 三组正常权限测试；
- 三组越权访问测试，并确认失败原因确为预期权限拒绝。

第四周数据库的多表查询、统计视图、完整性验证和数据库权限设计主体工作已经完成。

------

# 十四、本周阶段结论

第四周没有继续扩大数据库业务规模，而是在已有关系模式和样例数据基础上，将数据库从“能够保存和修改数据”进一步扩展为：

```
能够回答经营问题
        ↓
能够复用常见统计结果
        ↓
能够拒绝已由约束覆盖的典型非法记录
        ↓
能够限制不同角色对主要对象和字段的操作范围
```

多表查询验证了销售、商品、会员、库存、IP、预售等业务数据之间的关联能够支持实际经营分析。

统计视图将部分高频查询封装为稳定的数据访问方式。

完整性实验通过合法和非法数据正反例，验证了当前已由 `CHECK`、`UNIQUE`、`FOREIGN KEY` 和复合外键覆盖的数据规则能够实际发挥作用，并补齐了若干状态、时间和预售身份一致性约束。反例测试同时核对实际错误号和对应约束名，避免把其他原因造成的执行失败误判为测试通过。

权限实验将现实经营角色进一步映射为 SQL Server 数据库角色，通过最小权限授权和正常/越权测试，验证了不同员工在当前对象级、列级权限范围内能够完成典型操作，并会被拒绝访问未授权对象或字段。复杂状态迁移和跨表业务规则将在后续数据库编程与事务阶段继续收紧。越权测试进一步核对 SQL Server 权限拒绝错误及目标对象，确保测试失败原因与预期一致。

因此，目前第四周“连接、视图、完整性与授权”的主体业务工作已经形成完整闭环。

## 十二、阶段复现与重复运行

第一阶段完整复现统一按照：

```
00_create_database.sql
→ 01_create_tables.sql
→ 02_insert_sample_data.sql
→ 03_crud.sql
→ 04_query.sql
→ 05_view.sql
→ 06_constraint.sql
→ 07_role.sql
```

执行。`00` 在数据库不存在时创建数据库、存在时直接复用；`01` 会删除本项目已有视图和业务表并按依赖关系重新建表，因此完整执行链可以在已有数据库上再次运行并回到确定的样例初始状态。`05` 使用 `CREATE OR ALTER VIEW`，`06` 的补充约束带存在性判断，`07` 的角色、测试用户和角色成员关系也带存在性判断或可重复授权逻辑。

这里的重复运行是课程实验的“完整重建”语义：重新执行 `01` 会清空当前业务表数据。若只是希望保留已有业务数据，不应从 `01` 开始重建。

 620:     -- 预售采购必须明确关联一场预售活动
 621:     CONSTRAINT CK_PurchaseOrder_PresaleRequired
 622:         CHECK (
 623:             purchase_type <> 'PRESALE'
 624:             OR presale_id IS NOT NULL
 625:         ),
 626: 
 627:     -- RECEIVED 与验收时间 / 验收员工保持一致：
 628:     -- 完成验收后二者均存在；其他状态下二者均为空。
 629:     CONSTRAINT CK_PurchaseOrder_ReceivedInfo
 630:         CHECK (
 631:             (
 632:                 purchase_status = 'RECEIVED'
 633:                 AND received_time IS NOT NULL
 634:                 AND received_by IS NOT NULL
 635:             )
 636:             OR
 637:             (
 638:                 purchase_status <> 'RECEIVED'
 639:                 AND received_time IS NULL
 640:                 AND received_by IS NULL
 641:             )
 642:         ),
 643: 
 644:     CONSTRAINT CK_PurchaseOrder_ExpectedArrival
 645:         CHECK (
 646:             expected_arrival IS NULL
 647:             OR expected_arrival >= order_time
 648:         ),
 649: 
 650:     CONSTRAINT CK_PurchaseOrder_ReceivedTime
 651:         CHECK (
 652:             received_time IS NULL
 653:             OR received_time >= order_time
 654:         )
 655: );
 656: GO

 659: /* =========================================================
 660:    13. PurchaseOrderItem 采购订单明细
 661:    ========================================================= */
 662: 
 663: CREATE TABLE dbo.PurchaseOrderItem
 664: (
 665:     purchase_id INT NOT NULL,
 666:     product_id INT NOT NULL,
 667: 
 668:     ordered_qty INT NOT NULL,
 669:     unit_cost DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
 670: 
 671:     received_qty INT NOT NULL
 672:         CONSTRAINT DF_PurchaseOrderItem_ReceivedQty
 673:         DEFAULT (0),
 674: 
 675:     CONSTRAINT PK_PurchaseOrderItem
 676:         PRIMARY KEY (purchase_id, product_id),
 677: 
 678:     CONSTRAINT FK_PurchaseOrderItem_PurchaseOrder
 679:         FOREIGN KEY (purchase_id)
 680:         REFERENCES dbo.PurchaseOrder(purchase_id),
 681: 
 682:     CONSTRAINT FK_PurchaseOrderItem_Product
 683:         FOREIGN KEY (product_id)
 684:         REFERENCES dbo.Product(product_id),
 685: 
 686:     CONSTRAINT CK_PurchaseOrderItem_OrderedQty
 687:         CHECK (ordered_qty > 0),
 688: 
 689:     CONSTRAINT CK_PurchaseOrderItem_UnitCost
 690:         CHECK (unit_cost > 0),
 691: 
 692:     CONSTRAINT CK_PurchaseOrderItem_ReceivedQty
 693:         CHECK (
 694:             received_qty >= 0
 695:             AND received_qty <= ordered_qty
 696:         )
 697: );
 698: GO
 699: 
 700: 
 701: /* =========================================================
 702:    14. SalesOrder 销售订单
 703:    ========================================================= */
 704: 
 705: CREATE TABLE dbo.SalesOrder
 706: (
 707:     order_id INT NOT NULL,
 708:     order_no VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
 709: 
 710:     order_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
 711: 
 712:     member_id INT NULL,
 713:     cashier_id INT NOT NULL,
 714: 
 715:     reservation_id INT NULL,
 716: 
 717:     order_time DATETIME2 NULL,
 718: 
 719:     order_status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL
 720:         CONSTRAINT DF_SalesOrder_Status
 721:         DEFAULT ('CREATED'),
 722: 
 723:     CONSTRAINT PK_SalesOrder
 724:         PRIMARY KEY (order_id),
 725: 
 726:     CONSTRAINT UQ_SalesOrder_OrderNo
 727:         UNIQUE (order_no),
 728: 
 729:     CONSTRAINT FK_SalesOrder_Member
 730:         FOREIGN KEY (member_id)
 731:         REFERENCES dbo.Member(member_id),
 732: 
 733:     CONSTRAINT FK_SalesOrder_Cashier
 734:         FOREIGN KEY (cashier_id)
 735:         REFERENCES dbo.Employee(employee_id),
 736: 
 737:     CONSTRAINT FK_SalesOrder_PresaleReservation
 738:         FOREIGN KEY (reservation_id)
 739:         REFERENCES dbo.PresaleReservation(reservation_id),
 740: 
 741:     CONSTRAINT FK_SalesOrder_ReservationMember
 742:         FOREIGN KEY (reservation_id, member_id)
 743:         REFERENCES dbo.PresaleReservation(reservation_id, member_id),
 744: 
 745:     CONSTRAINT CK_SalesOrder_OrderID
 746:         CHECK (order_id > 0),
 747: 
 748:     CONSTRAINT CK_SalesOrder_Type
 749:         CHECK (
 750:             order_type IN ('SPOT', 'PRESALE')
 751:         ),
 752: 
 753:     CONSTRAINT CK_SalesOrder_Status
 754:         CHECK (
 755:             order_status IN
 756:             ('CREATED', 'COMPLETED', 'CANCELLED')
 757:         ),
 758: 
 759:     CONSTRAINT CK_SalesOrder_StatusTime
 760:         CHECK (
 761:             (order_status = 'COMPLETED' AND order_time IS NOT NULL)
 762:             OR
 763:             (order_status <> 'COMPLETED' AND order_time IS NULL)
 764:         ),
 765: 
 766:     /*
 767:        SPOT：
 768:        reservation_id 必须为空；
 769:        member_id 可以为空，也可以指向会员。
 770: 
 771:        PRESALE：
 772:        reservation_id 和 member_id 均不能为空。
 773:     */
 774:     CONSTRAINT CK_SalesOrder_TypeFields
 775:         CHECK
 776:         (
 777:             (
 778:                 order_type = 'SPOT'
 779:                 AND reservation_id IS NULL
 780:             )
 781:             OR
 782:             (
 783:                 order_type = 'PRESALE'
 784:                 AND reservation_id IS NOT NULL
 785:                 AND member_id IS NOT NULL
 786:             )
 787:         )
 788: );
 789: GO
 790: 
 791: 
 792: /*
 793:    一条非空 reservation_id
 794:    最多只能对应一笔销售订单。
 795: 
 796:    不能直接给 reservation_id 加普通 UNIQUE，
 797:    否则会影响多笔 reservation_id = NULL 的普通订单。
 798: */
 799: CREATE UNIQUE INDEX UX_SalesOrder_ReservationID

section='''## 十二、阶段复现与重复运行\n\n第一阶段完整复现统一按照：\n\n```\n00_create_database.sql\n→ 01_create_tables.sql\n→ 02_insert_sample_data.sql\n→ 03_crud.sql\n→ 04_query.sql\n→ 05_view.sql\n→ 06_constraint.sql\n→ 07_role.sql\n```\n\n执行。`00` 在数据库不存在时创建数据库、存在时直接复用；`01` 会删除本项目已有视图和业务表并按依赖关系重新建表，因此完整执行链可以在已有数据库上再次运行并回到确定的样例初始状态。`05` 使用 `CREATE OR ALTER VIEW`，`06` 的补充约束带存在性判断，`07` 的角色、测试用户和角色成员关系也带存在性判断或可重复授权逻辑。\n\n这里的重复运行是课程实验的“完整重建”语义：重新执行 `01` 会清空当前业务表数据。若只是希望保留已有业务数据，不应从 `01` 开始重建。\n\n------\n\n'''
# Remove existing trailing copy

if idx!=-1:

# Insert before current results
anchor='# 十三、当前第四周实验结果'
if anchor not in s:
    raise SystemExit('anchor missing')
s=s.replace(anchor,section+anchor,1)
p.write_text(s,encoding='utf-8')

if needle in s:

p.write_bytes(s.encode('gb18030'))

# Lightly correct SQL comment about multi-product scope
p=Path('/mnt/data/v4work/02_insert_sample_data.sql')

from pathlib import Path
import re, json
root=Path('/mnt/data/v4work')

def readsql(name):
    b=(root/name).read_bytes()
    for e in ['utf-8-sig','utf-8','gb18030']:
        try:return b.decode(e)
        except UnicodeDecodeError: pass
    raise ValueError(name)

s=readsql('01_create_tables.sql')
creates=re.findall(r'CREATE TABLE\s+dbo\.([A-Za-z0-9_]+)',s,re.I)
drops=re.findall(r'DROP TABLE IF EXISTS\s+dbo\.([A-Za-z0-9_]+)',s,re.I)
views_drop=re.findall(r'DROP VIEW IF EXISTS\s+dbo\.([A-Za-z0-9_]+)',s,re.I)
# table block and FK targets
edges=[]
for m in re.finditer(r'CREATE TABLE\s+dbo\.([A-Za-z0-9_]+)\s*\((.*?)(?=\n\);\s*\nGO)',s,re.S|re.I):
    src=m.group(1); block=m.group(2)
    for tgt in re.findall(r'REFERENCES\s+dbo\.([A-Za-z0-9_]+)\s*\(',block,re.I):
        edges.append((src,tgt))

create_pos={x:i for i,x in enumerate(creates)}
drop_pos={x:i for i,x in enumerate(drops)}
create_bad=[(a,b) for a,b in edges if create_pos.get(b,999)>=create_pos.get(a,-1)]
drop_bad=[(a,b) for a,b in edges if drop_pos.get(a,999)>=drop_pos.get(b,-1)] # child must drop earlier -> smaller index

print('tables_create',len(creates),creates)
print('tables_drop',len(drops),drops)
print('views_drop',len(views_drop),views_drop)
print('fk_edges',len(edges),edges)
print('bad_create_order',create_bad)
print('bad_drop_order',drop_bad)

v=readsql('05_view.sql')
print('create_or_alter_views',re.findall(r'CREATE OR ALTER VIEW\s+dbo\.([A-Za-z0-9_]+)',v,re.I))
print('plain_create_views',re.findall(r'(?<!OR ALTER )CREATE VIEW\s+dbo\.([A-Za-z0-9_]+)',v,re.I))

c=readsql('06_constraint.sql')
constraint_names=set(re.findall(r'CONSTRAINT\s+([A-Za-z0-9_]+)',s,re.I)) | set(re.findall(r'ADD CONSTRAINT\s+([A-Za-z0-9_]+)',c,re.I))
expected_names=set(re.findall(r"LIKE\s+N?'%([A-Za-z0-9_]+)%'",c,re.I))
print('constraint test names missing from DDL:',sorted(expected_names-constraint_names))
print('T tests',sorted(set(re.findall(r'\bT(\d+)\b',c)), key=int))

r=readsql('07_role.sql')
print('roles guarded',len(re.findall(r"IF DATABASE_PRINCIPAL_ID\(N'dbrole_",r)))
print('users guarded',len(re.findall(r"IF DATABASE_PRINCIPAL_ID\(N'u_test_",r)))
print('membership guarded',len(re.findall(r'IF NOT EXISTS\s*\(\s*SELECT 1\s*FROM sys\.database_role_members',r,re.S|re.I)))

# Every SQL points at same db (00 creates it)
for p in sorted(root.glob('*.sql')):
    x=readsql(p.name)
    uses=re.findall(r'USE\s+([A-Za-z0-9_]+)',x,re.I)
    print(p.name,'USE',uses)

from pathlib import Path
import re, ast, datetime
root=Path('/mnt/data/v4work')
s=(root/'02_insert_sample_data.sql').read_bytes().decode('gb18030')
# remove line comments only
s_no=re.sub(r'--.*','',s)
# simple scanner for INSERT INTO dbo.Table (cols) VALUES ... ;
pat=re.compile(r'INSERT INTO\s+dbo\.(\w+)\s*\((.*?)\)\s*VALUES\s*(.*?);\s*GO',re.S|re.I)

def split_top(text, sep=','):
    parts=[];cur=[];depth=0;ins=False;i=0
    while i<len(text):
        c=text[i]
        if ins:
            cur.append(c)
            if c=="'":
                if i+1<len(text) and text[i+1]=="'": cur.append(text[i+1]); i+=1
                else: ins=False
        else:
            if c=="'": ins=True;cur.append(c)
            elif c=='(': depth+=1;cur.append(c)
            elif c==')': depth-=1;cur.append(c)
            elif c==sep and depth==0: parts.append(''.join(cur).strip());cur=[]
            else: cur.append(c)
        i+=1
    if ''.join(cur).strip(): parts.append(''.join(cur).strip())
    return parts

def parse_val(v):
    v=v.strip()
    if v.upper()=='NULL': return None
    if v.startswith("N'"): v=v[1:]
    if v.startswith("'") and v.endswith("'"): return v[1:-1].replace("''", "'")
    try:
        return int(v) if re.fullmatch(r'[+-]?\d+',v) else float(v)
    except: return v

data={}
for m in pat.finditer(s_no):
    t=m.group(1); cols=[x.strip() for x in m.group(2).split(',')]
    vals=m.group(3).strip()
    tuples=[]; cur=[]; depth=0;ins=False;start=None;i=0
    while i<len(vals):
        c=vals[i]
        if ins:
            if c=="'":
                if i+1<len(vals) and vals[i+1]=="'": i+=1
                else: ins=False
        else:
            if c=="'": ins=True
            elif c=='(':
                if depth==0:start=i+1
                depth+=1
            elif c==')':
                depth-=1
                if depth==0 and start is not None:
                    content=vals[start:i]
                    arr=[parse_val(x) for x in split_top(content)]
                    tuples.append(dict(zip(cols,arr)))
                    if len(arr)!=len(cols): print('len mismatch',t,len(cols),len(arr),content)
                    start=None
        i+=1
    data[t]=tuples
print({k:len(v) for k,v in data.items()})
errs=[]
def uniq(table,col):
 vals=[r[col] for r in data[table] if r[col] is not None]
 if len(vals)!=len(set(vals)): errs.append(f'{table}.{col} duplicate')
for t,pk in [('ProductCategory','category_id'),('AnimeIP','ip_id'),('BusinessRole','role_id'),('Member','member_id'),('Supplier','supplier_id'),('CharacterInfo','character_id'),('Employee','employee_id'),('Product','product_id'),('Inventory','product_id'),('PresaleActivity','presale_id'),('PresaleReservation','reservation_id'),('PurchaseOrder','purchase_id'),('SalesOrder','order_id')]: uniq(t,pk)
for t,c in [('ProductCategory','category_name'),('AnimeIP','ip_name'),('BusinessRole','role_name'),('Member','member_no'),('Member','phone'),('Supplier','supplier_code'),('Employee','employee_no'),('Product','product_code'),('PresaleActivity','presale_code'),('PresaleReservation','reservation_no'),('PurchaseOrder','purchase_no'),('SalesOrder','order_no')]:uniq(t,c)
# lookup dicts
D={t:{next(iter(r.values())):r for r in rows} for t,rows in data.items() if rows}
# explicit maps
maps={t:{r[list(r)[0]]:r for r in rows} for t,rows in data.items()}
# FKs / checks
ip_ids={r['ip_id'] for r in data['AnimeIP']}; cat_ids={r['category_id'] for r in data['ProductCategory']}; role_ids={r['role_id'] for r in data['BusinessRole']}; member_ids={r['member_id'] for r in data['Member']}; supplier_ids={r['supplier_id'] for r in data['Supplier']}; emp_ids={r['employee_id'] for r in data['Employee']}; prod_ids={r['product_id'] for r in data['Product']}; presale_ids={r['presale_id'] for r in data['PresaleActivity']}; reserv_ids={r['reservation_id'] for r in data['PresaleReservation']}; po_ids={r['purchase_id'] for r in data['PurchaseOrder']}; so_ids={r['order_id'] for r in data['SalesOrder']}
chars={(r['character_id'],r['ip_id']) for r in data['CharacterInfo']}; char_ids={r['character_id'] for r in data['CharacterInfo']}
for r in data['CharacterInfo']:
 if r['ip_id'] not in ip_ids: errs.append('Character bad ip')
for r in data['Employee']:
 if r['role_id'] not in role_ids: errs.append('Employee bad role')
for r in data['Product']:
 if r['category_id'] not in cat_ids: errs.append('Product bad cat')
 if r['ip_id'] is not None and r['ip_id'] not in ip_ids: errs.append('Product bad ip')
 if r['character_id'] is not None and r['character_id'] not in char_ids: errs.append('Product bad char')
 if r['character_id'] is not None and (r['character_id'],r['ip_id']) not in chars: errs.append(f"Product {r['product_id']} char/ip mismatch")
 if not r['sale_price']>0: errs.append('bad price')
for r in data['Inventory']:
 if r['product_id'] not in prod_ids: errs.append('Inventory bad prod')
 if not (r['on_hand_qty']>=0 and r['reserved_qty']>=0 and r['reorder_point']>=0 and r['reserved_qty']<=r['on_hand_qty']): errs.append('Inventory check')
for r in data['PresaleActivity']:
 if r['product_id'] not in prod_ids or r['created_by'] not in emp_ids: errs.append('Presale fk')
 st=datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(r['start_time']); en=datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(r['end_time'])
 if not st<en: errs.append('Presale time')
 if r['pickup_deadline'] is not None and not datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(r['pickup_deadline'])>en: errs.append('pickup deadline')
 if not (r['presale_unit_price']>0 and r['deposit_per_unit']>=0 and r['deposit_per_unit']<=r['presale_unit_price']): errs.append('presale money')
for r in data['PresaleReservation']:
 if r['presale_id'] not in presale_ids or r['member_id'] not in member_ids: errs.append('res fk')
 if r['quantity']<=0 or r['deposit_paid']<0 or r['deposit_refunded']<0 or r['deposit_refunded']>r['deposit_paid']: errs.append(f"res numeric {r['reservation_id']}")
 cancelled=r['reservation_status']=='CANCELLED'
 if cancelled != (r['cancelled_at'] is not None): errs.append(f"cancel info {r['reservation_id']}")
for r in data['PurchaseOrder']:
 if r['supplier_id'] not in supplier_ids or r['created_by'] not in emp_ids or (r['presale_id'] is not None and r['presale_id'] not in presale_ids) or (r['received_by'] is not None and r['received_by'] not in emp_ids): errs.append('po fk')
 if r['purchase_type']=='PRESALE' and r['presale_id'] is None: errs.append('po presale missing')
 recv=r['purchase_status']=='RECEIVED'
 if recv != (r['received_time'] is not None and r['received_by'] is not None): errs.append(f"po received info {r['purchase_id']}")
 ot=datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(r['order_time'])
 if r['expected_arrival'] is not None and datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(r['expected_arrival'])<ot: errs.append('po expected time')
 if r['received_time'] is not None and datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(r['received_time'])<ot: errs.append('po recv time')
for r in data['PurchaseOrderItem']:
 if r['purchase_id'] not in po_ids or r['product_id'] not in prod_ids: errs.append('poi fk')
 if not (r['ordered_qty']>0 and r['unit_cost']>0 and 0<=r['received_qty']<=r['ordered_qty']): errs.append('poi numeric')
# reservation member map
rm={r['reservation_id']:r['member_id'] for r in data['PresaleReservation']}
seen_nonnull=[]
for r in data['SalesOrder']:
 if r['member_id'] is not None and r['member_id'] not in member_ids: errs.append('so member')
 if r['cashier_id'] not in emp_ids: errs.append('so cashier')
 if r['reservation_id'] is not None and r['reservation_id'] not in reserv_ids: errs.append('so reservation')
 if r['reservation_id'] is not None:
  seen_nonnull.append(r['reservation_id'])
  if rm[r['reservation_id']]!=r['member_id']: errs.append(f"so res/member mismatch {r['order_id']}")
 completed=r['order_status']=='COMPLETED'
 if completed != (r['order_time'] is not None): errs.append(f"so time {r['order_id']}")
 if r['order_type']=='SPOT' and r['reservation_id'] is not None: errs.append('spot reservation')
 if r['order_type']=='PRESALE' and (r['reservation_id'] is None or r['member_id'] is None): errs.append('presale fields')
if len(seen_nonnull)!=len(set(seen_nonnull)): errs.append('duplicate nonnull reservation salesorder')
for r in data['SalesOrderItem']:
 if r['order_id'] not in so_ids or r['product_id'] not in prod_ids: errs.append('soi fk')
 if not (r['quantity']>0 and r['unit_price']>0): errs.append('soi numeric')
# composite PK checks
for t,cols in [('CharacterInfo',['ip_id','character_name']),('PurchaseOrderItem',['purchase_id','product_id']),('SalesOrderItem',['order_id','product_id'])]:
 vals=[tuple(r[c] for c in cols) for r in data[t]]
 if len(vals)!=len(set(vals)): errs.append(f'{t} composite dup')
print('validation_errors:',errs)

## Tool
*2026-10-02 03:18*
/* =========================================================
   04_query.sql
   二次元周边商品店：第四周业务查询

   执行前：
   00_create_database.sql
   01_create_tables.sql
   02_insert_sample_data.sql
   ========================================================= */

USE AnimeGoodsStoreDB;
GO


/* =========================================================
   Q1. 查看完整销售订单明细

   业务问题：
   查看每笔订单由谁结账、会员是谁、购买了什么商品、
   数量、成交单价及明细金额。

   使用 LEFT JOIN Member：
   因为普通非会员顾客的 member_id 可以为 NULL。
   ========================================================= */

SELECT
    so.order_no,
    so.order_type,
    so.order_status,
    so.order_time,

    m.member_no,
    m.member_name,

    e.employee_no AS cashier_no,
    e.employee_name AS cashier_name,

    p.product_code,
    p.product_name,

    soi.quantity,
    soi.unit_price,
    soi.quantity * soi.unit_price AS item_amount
FROM dbo.SalesOrder AS so
INNER JOIN dbo.SalesOrderItem AS soi
    ON so.order_id = soi.order_id
INNER JOIN dbo.Product AS p
    ON soi.product_id = p.product_id
INNER JOIN dbo.Employee AS e
    ON so.cashier_id = e.employee_id
LEFT JOIN dbo.Member AS m
    ON so.member_id = m.member_id
ORDER BY so.order_id, p.product_id;
GO


/* =========================================================
   Q2. 统计每种商品的累计销量和销售额

   只把 COMPLETED 订单计入实际销量。

   从 Product 出发并使用 LEFT JOIN，
   使尚未产生销售记录的商品仍然可以显示为 0。
   ========================================================= */

SELECT
    p.product_id,
    p.product_code,
    p.product_name,

    SUM(
        CASE
            WHEN so.order_status = 'COMPLETED'
            THEN soi.quantity
            ELSE 0
        END
    ) AS total_sales_qty,

    SUM(
        CASE
            WHEN so.order_status = 'COMPLETED'
            THEN soi.quantity * soi.unit_price
            ELSE 0
        END
    ) AS total_sales_amount

FROM dbo.Product AS p
LEFT JOIN dbo.SalesOrderItem AS soi
    ON p.product_id = soi.product_id
LEFT JOIN dbo.SalesOrder AS so
    ON soi.order_id = so.order_id
GROUP BY
    p.product_id,
    p.product_code,
    p.product_name
ORDER BY total_sales_qty DESC, p.product_id;
GO


/* =========================================================
   Q3. 按 IP 统计累计销量和销售额

   用于分析不同作品 / IP 的销售表现。
   ========================================================= */

SELECT
    ip.ip_id,
    ip.ip_name,

    SUM(
        CASE
            WHEN so.order_status = 'COMPLETED'
            THEN soi.quantity
            ELSE 0
        END
    ) AS total_sales_qty,

    SUM(
        CASE
            WHEN so.order_status = 'COMPLETED'
            THEN soi.quantity * soi.unit_price
            ELSE 0
        END
    ) AS total_sales_amount

FROM dbo.AnimeIP AS ip
LEFT JOIN dbo.Product AS p
    ON ip.ip_id = p.ip_id
LEFT JOIN dbo.SalesOrderItem AS soi
    ON p.product_id = soi.product_id
LEFT JOIN dbo.SalesOrder AS so
    ON soi.order_id = so.order_id
GROUP BY
    ip.ip_id,
    ip.ip_name
ORDER BY total_sales_amount DESC;
GO


/* =========================================================
   Q4. 按商品类别统计累计销量和销售额

   与 IP 统计角度不同：
   这里分析徽章、亚克力制品、玩偶、色纸等商品类别。
   ========================================================= */

SELECT
    pc.category_id,
    pc.category_name,

    SUM(
        CASE
            WHEN so.order_status = 'COMPLETED'
            THEN soi.quantity
            ELSE 0
        END
    ) AS total_sales_qty,

    SUM(
        CASE
            WHEN so.order_status = 'COMPLETED'
            THEN soi.quantity * soi.unit_price
            ELSE 0
        END
    ) AS total_sales_amount

FROM dbo.ProductCategory AS pc
LEFT JOIN dbo.Product AS p
    ON pc.category_id = p.category_id
LEFT JOIN dbo.SalesOrderItem AS soi
    ON p.product_id = soi.product_id
LEFT JOIN dbo.SalesOrder AS so
    ON soi.order_id = so.order_id
GROUP BY
    pc.category_id,
    pc.category_name
ORDER BY total_sales_amount DESC;
GO


/* =========================================================
   Q5. 统计每个会员的消费次数和累计消费金额

   要求：
   即使会员尚未产生任何消费，也要显示出来。

   COUNT(DISTINCT so.order_id)：
   避免一张包含多个商品的订单被重复计算为多笔订单。
   ========================================================= */

SELECT
    m.member_id,
    m.member_no,
    m.member_name,

    COUNT(DISTINCT so.order_id) AS completed_order_count,

    COALESCE(
        SUM(soi.quantity * soi.unit_price),
        0
    ) AS total_consumption

FROM dbo.Member AS m
LEFT JOIN dbo.SalesOrder AS so
    ON m.member_id = so.member_id
   AND so.order_status = 'COMPLETED'
LEFT JOIN dbo.SalesOrderItem AS soi
    ON so.order_id = soi.order_id
GROUP BY
    m.member_id,
    m.member_no,
    m.member_name
ORDER BY total_consumption DESC;
GO


/* =========================================================
   Q6. 查询当前达到或低于补货警戒值的商品

   可销售库存：
   available_qty = on_hand_qty - reserved_qty
   ========================================================= */

SELECT
    p.product_id,
    p.product_code,
    p.product_name,

    i.on_hand_qty,
    i.reserved_qty,
    i.on_hand_qty - i.reserved_qty AS available_qty,
    i.reorder_point

FROM dbo.Product AS p
INNER JOIN dbo.Inventory AS i
    ON p.product_id = i.product_id

WHERE
    i.on_hand_qty - i.reserved_qty <= i.reorder_point

ORDER BY
    available_qty ASC,
    p.product_id;
GO


/* =========================================================
   Q7. 统计各预售活动当前有效预订需求

   ACTIVE 表示当前仍然有效、尚未取消、
   尚未完成提货的预订。

   同时显示 decision_demand_qty，
   用于与采购决策时保存的历史需求快照比较。
   ========================================================= */

SELECT
    pa.presale_id,
    pa.presale_code,
    p.product_code,
    p.product_name,
    pa.presale_status,

    SUM(
        CASE
            WHEN pr.reservation_status = 'ACTIVE'
            THEN pr.quantity
            ELSE 0
        END
    ) AS current_active_demand_qty,

    pa.decision_demand_qty,
    pa.extra_stock_qty,
    pa.planned_purchase_qty

FROM dbo.PresaleActivity AS pa
INNER JOIN dbo.Product AS p
    ON pa.product_id = p.product_id
LEFT JOIN dbo.PresaleReservation AS pr
    ON pa.presale_id = pr.presale_id
GROUP BY
    pa.presale_id,
    pa.presale_code,
    p.product_code,
    p.product_name,
    pa.presale_status,
    pa.decision_demand_qty,
    pa.extra_stock_qty,
    pa.planned_purchase_qty
ORDER BY pa.presale_id;
GO


/* =========================================================
   Q8. 查询累计销量不少于 2 件的商品

   使用 HAVING：
   因为筛选条件作用于 SUM(quantity) 聚合结果，
   而不是某一条原始记录。
   ========================================================= */

SELECT
    p.product_id,
    p.product_code,
    p.product_name,
    SUM(soi.quantity) AS total_sales_qty,
    SUM(soi.quantity * soi.unit_price) AS total_sales_amount

FROM dbo.Product AS p
INNER JOIN dbo.SalesOrderItem AS soi
    ON p.product_id = soi.product_id
INNER JOIN dbo.SalesOrder AS so
    ON soi.order_id = so.order_id

WHERE so.order_status = 'COMPLETED'

GROUP BY
    p.product_id,
    p.product_code,
    p.product_name

HAVING SUM(soi.quantity) >= 2

ORDER BY total_sales_qty DESC;
GO


/* =========================================================
   Q9. 查询累计消费金额高于会员平均消费金额的会员

   第一步：
   计算每个有实际消费记录会员的累计消费金额。

   第二步：
   对这些会员的累计消费额求平均值。

   第三步：
   找出高于该平均值的会员。
   ========================================================= */

SELECT
    mc.member_id,
    mc.member_no,
    mc.member_name,
    mc.total_consumption

FROM
(
    SELECT
        m.member_id,
        m.member_no,
        m.member_name,
        SUM(soi.quantity * soi.unit_price) AS total_consumption

    FROM dbo.Member AS m
    INNER JOIN dbo.SalesOrder AS so
        ON m.member_id = so.member_id
    INNER JOIN dbo.SalesOrderItem AS soi
        ON so.order_id = soi.order_id

    WHERE so.order_status = 'COMPLETED'

    GROUP BY
        m.member_id,
        m.member_no,
        m.member_name
) AS mc

WHERE mc.total_consumption >
(
    SELECT AVG(CAST(member_total.total_consumption AS DECIMAL(18,2)))
    FROM
    (
        SELECT
            so2.member_id,
            SUM(
                soi2.quantity * soi2.unit_price
            ) AS total_consumption

        FROM dbo.SalesOrder AS so2
        INNER JOIN dbo.SalesOrderItem AS soi2
            ON so2.order_id = soi2.order_id

        WHERE
            so2.order_status = 'COMPLETED'
            AND so2.member_id IS NOT NULL

        GROUP BY so2.member_id
    ) AS member_total
)

ORDER BY mc.total_consumption DESC;
GO

### 这次对“初次运行 / 已有库再次运行”的处理

我没有新增 reset 脚本，也没有让 `00_create_database.sql` 每次直接 `DROP DATABASE`，而是采用了更适合当前课程实验的方案：

- `00_create_database.sql`
  - 数据库不存在 → 创建。
  - 数据库已经存在 → 直接复用。
  - 输出明确提示，便于你运行时判断当前是哪种情况。

- `01_create_tables.sql`
  - 现在承担“**模式重建入口**”的职责。
  - 首先删除当前项目的 4 个统计视图。
  - 然后严格按照外键依赖的**逆序**删除 15 张业务表。
  - 最后重新创建全部 15 张表和基础约束。
  - 因此第一次执行和已有数据库第二次完整执行，都可以走相同的：
    `00 → 01 → ... → 07`。

我专门静态计算了所有外键依赖关系，共检查到 **23 条 FK 依赖**，当前表的创建顺序和删除顺序都不存在逆序错误。

需要注意的是：**重新执行 `01` 会清空这 15 张业务表中的现有数据。** 这是有意设计成课程实验中的“确定性重建”，而不是生产数据库的增量升级方案。

- `05_view.sql`
  - 4 个视图全部从 `CREATE VIEW` 改为：
    ```sql
    CREATE OR ALTER VIEW
    ```
  - 所以即使单独再次执行 `05`，也不会因为视图已经存在而报错。

- `06_constraint.sql`
  - 原先补充约束就已经带 `IF NOT EXISTS`，无需再改。
  - T1–T14 全部使用事务并回滚。

- `07_role.sql`
  - 数据库角色、测试用户、角色成员关系原先已经带存在性判断。
  - `GRANT` 本身可以重新执行。
  - 因此即使第二轮中角色和测试用户仍然存在，也能正常继续。
  - `01` 重建表/视图后旧对象权限消失，`07` 会重新对新对象授权。

同时我在第三、第四周说明中加入了非常简短的重复运行说明，没有把文档扩成一大段错误处理手册。
